\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{compute\_elec\_add()}{compute\_elec\_add()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceqpwf__module_af3dcd307e2e32ac07e5e778e5ecd1ea5} 
subroutine qpwf\+\_\+module\+::compute\+\_\+elec\+\_\+add (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{double precision, dimension(3, \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{rnew}{, }\item[{integer}]{nel\+\_\+read}{, }\item[{integer}]{nelup\+\_\+read}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}4}]{signpsi}{, }\item[{double precision}]{spsiln}{, }\item[{double precision}]{shiftlog}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Computes quasi-\/particle wave function for electron addition. 

This subroutine computes the quasi-\/particle wave function for electron addition by evaluating the wave function at grid points. It handles both real and complex wavefunctions and computes wave function ratios for each grid point. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em rnew} & Electron positions (3, nel\+\_\+read) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nel\+\_\+read} & Number of electrons to read \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelup\+\_\+read} & Number of up-\/spin electrons to read \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em signpsi} & Sign of the wave function \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em spsiln} & Logarithm of the wave function \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em shiftlog} & Logarithmic shift parameter \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{compute\_elec\_delete()}{compute\_elec\_delete()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceqpwf__module_ad8ff16b569e8720193aa6657cddb1f13} 
subroutine qpwf\+\_\+module\+::compute\+\_\+elec\+\_\+delete (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{double precision, dimension(3, \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{rnew}{, }\item[{integer}]{nel\+\_\+read}{, }\item[{integer}]{nelup\+\_\+read}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}4}]{signpsi}{, }\item[{double precision}]{spsiln}{, }\item[{double precision}]{shiftlog}{, }\item[{integer, dimension(3)}]{mesh}{, }\item[{double precision, dimension(3)}]{vell}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Computes quasi-\/particle wave function for electron deletion. 

This subroutine computes the quasi-\/particle wave function for electron deletion by evaluating the wave function at grid points where electrons are removed. It handles both up and down electron deletions and computes wave function ratios for each grid point. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em rnew} & Electron positions (3, nel\+\_\+read) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nel\+\_\+read} & Number of electrons to read \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelup\+\_\+read} & Number of up-\/spin electrons to read \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em signpsi} & Sign of the wave function \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em spsiln} & Logarithm of the wave function \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em shiftlog} & Logarithmic shift parameter \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em mesh} & Grid mesh dimensions (3) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em vell} & Grid velocity vectors (3) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{compute\_extrapolate()}{compute\_extrapolate()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceqpwf__module_aa5ec0830c5d8eae99620050de12848e2} 
subroutine qpwf\+\_\+module\+::compute\+\_\+extrapolate (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{double precision, dimension(3, \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{rnew}{, }\item[{integer}]{nel\+\_\+read}{, }\item[{integer}]{nelup\+\_\+read}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}4}]{signpsi}{, }\item[{double precision}]{spsiln}{, }\item[{double precision}]{shiftlog}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Computes quasi-\/particle wave function by extrapolation. 

This subroutine computes the quasi-\/particle wave function by extrapolation from a reference configuration. It finds the closest electron to the extrapolation point and computes wave function values at different distances. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em rnew} & Electron positions (3, nel\+\_\+read) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nel\+\_\+read} & Number of electrons to read \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelup\+\_\+read} & Number of up-\/spin electrons to read \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em signpsi} & Sign of the wave function \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em spsiln} & Logarithm of the wave function \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em shiftlog} & Logarithmic shift parameter \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{compute\_qpwf\_k\_space()}{compute\_qpwf\_k\_space()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespaceqpwf__module_ab834bab6644f8982794af4a35a8dfd5f} 
subroutine qpwf\+\_\+module\+::compute\+\_\+qpwf\+\_\+k\+\_\+space (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{double precision, dimension(3, \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*})}]{rnew}{, }\item[{integer}]{nel\+\_\+read}{, }\item[{integer}]{nelup\+\_\+read}{, }\item[{real\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}4}]{signpsi}{, }\item[{double precision}]{spsiln}{, }\item[{double precision}]{shiftlog}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Computes quasi-\/particle wave function in k-\/space. 

This subroutine computes the quasi-\/particle wave function in k-\/space by evaluating plane wave contributions at grid points. It handles both up and down electron additions and computes real and imaginary parts of the wave function for each grid point. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em rnew} & Electron positions (3, nel\+\_\+read) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nel\+\_\+read} & Number of electrons to read \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em nelup\+\_\+read} & Number of up-\/spin electrons to read \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em signpsi} & Sign of the wave function \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em spsiln} & Logarithm of the wave function \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em shiftlog} & Logarithmic shift parameter \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
